     "dyn_identity": 0.0, "coord_identity": 0.0,\n            })\n            continue\n\n        obj = dim_to_obj[d]\n        z_d = z_dyn_pooled[:, d]\n        z_c = z_coord_arr[:, d]\n\n        r2_dyn_pos = fit_probe_r2(z_d, pos_arr[:, obj])\n        r2_coord_pos = fit_probe_r2(z_c, pos_arr[:, obj])\n\n        r2_dyn_ch = []\n        r2_coord_ch = []\n        for ch in range(3):\n            r2_dyn_ch.append(fit_probe_r2(z_d, colors_arr[:, obj, ch]))\n            r2_coord_ch.append(fit_probe_r2(z_c, colors_arr[:, obj, ch]))\n        r2_dyn_color = float(np.mean(r2_dyn_ch))\n        r2_coord_color = float(np.mean(r2_coord_ch))\n\n        max_radius = 20.0\n        identity_vec = np.zeros((num_samples, 4))\n        identity_vec[:, :3] = colors_arr[:, obj, :]\n        identity_vec[:, 3] = radii_arr[:, obj] / max_radius\n\n        r2_dyn_identity = fit_multivariate_probe_r2(z_d, identity_vec)\n        r2_coord_identity = fit_multivariate_probe_r2(z_c, identity_vec)\n\n        r2_per_dim.append({\n            "dyn_color": r2_dyn_color,\n            "coord_color": r2_coord_color,\n            "dyn_pos": r2_dyn_pos,\n            "coord_pos": r2_coord_pos,\n            "dyn_identity": r2_dyn_identity,\n            "coord_identity": r2_coord_identity,\n        })\n\n    r2_dyn_color_all = np.mean([p["dyn_color"] for p in r2_per_dim]) if r2_per_dim else 0.0\n    r2_coord_color_all = np.mean([p["coord_color"] for p in r2_per_dim]) if r2_per_dim else 0.0\n    r2_dyn_pos_all = np.mean([p["dyn_pos"] for p in r2_per_dim]) if r2_per_dim else 0.0\n    r2_coord_pos_all = np.mean([p["coord_pos"] for p in r2_per_dim]) if r2_per_dim else 0.0\n    delta_r2_color = float(r2_dyn_color_all - r2_coord_color_all)\n\n    r2_dyn_identity_all = np.mean([p["dyn_identity"] for p in r2_per_dim]) if r2_per_dim else 0.0\n    r2_coord_identity_all = np.mean([p["coord_identity"] for p in r2_per_dim]) if r2_per_dim else 0.0\n    delta_r2_identity = float(r2_dyn_identity_all - r2_coord_identity_all)\n\n    return {\n        "dim_to_obj": dim_to_obj,\n        "r2_per_dim": r2_per_dim,\n        "r2_dyn_color": float(r2_dyn_color_all),\n        "r2_coord_color": float(r2_coord_color_all),\n        "r2_dyn_pos": float(r2_dyn_pos_all),\n        "r2_coord_pos": float(r2_coord_pos_all),\n        "delta_r2_color": delta_r2_color,\n        "r2_dyn_identity": float(r2_dyn_identity_all),\n        "r2_coord_identity": float(r2_coord_identity_all),\n        "delta_r2_identity": delta_r2_identity,\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_semantic_probes(model, num_samples=200, train_ratio=0.5,\n                            base_seed=30000, device="cpu"):\n    model.eval()\n    (traj_z_dyn, traj_z_coord, traj_pos, traj_colors, traj_radii,\n     z_dyn_arr, z_coord_arr, pos_arr, colors_arr, radii_arr) = \\\n        collect_multitraj_eval_data(model, num_samples=num_samples,\n                                     base_seed=base_seed, device=device)\n\n    d_t = model.d_t\n    sub_features = model.sub_features\n\n    return _compute_semantic_probes_core(\n        z_dyn_arr, z_coord_arr, pos_arr, colors_arr, radii_arr,\n        d_t, sub_features, train_ratio=train_ratio\n    )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Evaluation with diagnostics\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef evaluate_run(model, arm_config, seed, device, eval_steps=200):\n    d_t = arm_config.get("d_t", 3)\n    sub_features = arm_config.get("sub_features", 1)\n    name = arm_config["name"]\n\n    # --- Collapse check on eval samples ---\n    eval_env = PhysicsSandbox(N=3, seed=seed + 10000)\n    eval_history = collections.deque(maxlen=4)\n    obs = eval_env.reset()\n    eval_history.append(obs)\n    for _ in range(4):\n        obs, _ = eval_env.step({"acc": 0.0, "push": False})\n        eval_history.append(obs)\n\n    z_dyn_all = []\n    z_coord_all = []\n    for _ in range(eval_steps):\n        obs, _ = eval_env.step({"acc": 0.0, "push": False})\n        eval_history.append(obs)\n        if len(eval_history) == 4:\n            x_t = torch.from_numpy(eval_history[3]).float().unsqueeze(0).to(device)\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                z_coord, z_dyn = model.encoder(x_t)\n            z_dyn_all.append(z_dyn[:, :d_t * sub_features].cpu().numpy())\n            z_coord_all.append(z_coord[:, :d_t].cpu().numpy())\n\n    z_dyn_arr = np.concatenate(z_dyn_all, axis=0)\n    z_coord_arr = np.concatenate(z_coord_all, axis=0)\n\n    if sub_features > 1:\n        z_dyn_pooled_check = z_dyn_arr[:, :d_t * sub_features].reshape(eval_steps, d_t, sub_features).mean(axis=2)\n    else:\n        z_dyn_pooled_check = z_dyn_arr[:, :d_t]\n    has_collapsed_eval, per_dim_std = check_collapse(z_dyn_pooled_check, d_t)\n    vh = compute_vicreg_health(z_dyn_pooled_check, d_t)\n\n    # --- Centroid MSE ---\n    test_env = PhysicsSandbox(N=3, seed=seed + 20000)\n    test_history = collections.deque(maxlen=4)\n    obs = test_env.reset()\n    test_history.append(obs)\n    centroid_mse = compute_centroid_mse(model, test_env, test_history,\n                                         num_samples=eval_steps, device=device)\n\n    # --- Semantic probes (Hungarian only) ---\n    semantic = compute_semantic_probes(model, num_samples=eval_steps,\n                                       base_seed=seed + 30000, device=device)\n\n    results = {\n        "arm": name,\n        "seed": seed,\n        "collapsed_eval": bool(has_collapsed_eval),\n        "per_dim_std": per_dim_std.tolist(),\n        "vicreg_per_dim_std": per_dim_std.tolist(),\n        "vicreg_mean_abs_corr": vh["mean_abs_corr"],\n        "centroid_mse_mean": centroid_mse["mse_mean"],\n        "centroid_r_mean": centroid_mse["r_mean"],\n        "delta_r2_color": semantic["delta_r2_color"],\n        "r2_dyn_color": semantic["r2_dyn_color"],\n        "r2_coord_color": semantic["r2_coord_color"],\n        "r2_dyn_pos": semantic["r2_dyn_pos"],\n        "r2_coord_pos": semantic["r2_coord_pos"],\n        "r2_dyn_identity": semantic["r2_dyn_identity"],\n        "r2_coord_identity": semantic["r2_coord_identity"],\n        "delta_r2_identity": semantic["delta_r2_identity"],\n        "dim_to_obj": semantic["dim_to_obj"],\n    }\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Single-run training + evaluation\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef run_single(arm_config, seed, device, dry_run=False):\n    name = arm_config["name"]\n    sim_weight = arm_config.get("sim_weight", 25.0)\n    var_weight = arm_config.get("var_weight", 25.0)\n    cov_weight = arm_config.get("cov_weight", 25.0)\n    pos_encoding = arm_config.get("pos_encoding", "none")\n    d_t = arm_config.get("d_t", 3)\n    dyn_readout = arm_config.get("dyn_readout", "mean")\n    ccr_mode = arm_config.get("ccr_mode", "covariance")\n    ccr_smooth_weight = arm_config.get("ccr_smooth_weight", 10.0)\n    ccr_spatial_weight = arm_config.get("ccr_spatial_weight", 10.0)\n    d_max = arm_config.get("d_max", 8)\n    sub_features = arm_config.get("sub_features", 1)\n    lr = arm_config.get("lr", 3e-4)\n    batch_size = arm_config.get("batch_size", 32)\n    replay_buffer_capacity = arm_config.get("replay_buffer_capacity", 4000)\n    sim_weight_ramp = arm_config.get("sim_weight_ramp", False)\n    sim_weight_ramp_steps = arm_config.get("sim_weight_ramp_steps", 1000)\n\n    set_seed(seed)\n\n    total_steps = 5 if dry_run else 8000\n    eval_steps = min(200, total_steps // 2) if dry_run else 200\n\n    model = NonParametricJEPASpatial(\n        d_max=d_max,\n        h=3,\n        k=4,\n        cooldown=300,\n        stabilization_period=100,\n        pos_encoding=pos_encoding,\n        primary_objective="jepa",\n        gdasr_log_only=True,\n        dyn_readout=dyn_readout,\n        sub_features=sub_features,\n        dyn_source="spatial",\n    )\n    model.d_t = d_t\n    model = model.to(device)\n\n    optimizer = optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=lr)\n\n    env = PhysicsSandbox(N=3, seed=seed)\n    obs = env.reset()\n    history = collections.deque(maxlen=4)\n    history.append(obs)\n\n    replay_buffer = ExtendedReplayBuffer(capacity=replay_buffer_capacity)\n\n    def _prefill(n):\n        while len(replay_buffer) < n:\n            obs, info = env.step({"acc": 0.0, "push": False})\n            history.append(obs)\n            if len(history) == 4:\n                replay_buffer.push(\n                    np.stack(list(history)[:3], axis=0),\n                    history[3],\n                    info["positions"],\n                    info["colors"],\n                    info["radii"],\n                )\n\n    prefill_steps = max(1, min(total_steps - 2, 200))\n    _prefill(prefill_steps)\n\n    logs = []\n\n    for step in range(max(1, prefill_steps + 1), total_steps + 1):\n        obs, info = env.step({"acc": 0.0, "push": False})\n        history.append(obs)\n\n        if len(history) == 4:\n            replay_buffer.push(\n                np.stack(list(history)[:3], axis=0),\n                history[3],\n                info["positions"],\n                info["colors"],\n                info["radii"],\n            )\n\n        x_hist_b, x_target_b, pos_b, colors_b, radii_b = replay_buffer.sample(min(batch_size, len(replay_buffer)))\n        x_hist_t = torch.from_numpy(x_hist_b).float().to(device)\n        x_target_t = torch.from_numpy(x_target_b).float().to(device)\n        pos_t = torch.from_numpy(pos_b).float().to(device)\n        colors_t = torch.from_numpy(colors_b).float().to(device)\n\n        # Sim-weight ramp for A3\n        current_sim_weight = sim_weight\n        if sim_weight_ramp and step <= sim_weight_ramp_steps:\n            current_sim_weight = sim_weight * (step / float(sim_weight_ramp_steps))\n\n        model.train()\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\n\n        loss_dict, _, (z_target_coord, z_target_dyn) = model(\n            x_hist_t,\n            x_target_t,\n            sim_weight=current_sim_weight,\n            var_weight=var_weight,\n            cov_weight=cov_weight,\n            d_t_predict=d_t,\n            ccr_mode=ccr_mode,\n            ccr_smooth_weight=ccr_smooth_weight,\n            ccr_spatial_weight=ccr_spatial_weight,\n        )\n\n        total_loss = loss_dict["loss"]\n        total_loss.backward()\n        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)\n        optimizer.step()\n\n        sim_loss_val = loss_dict["sim_loss"].item()\n        model.update_recruitment_logic(sim_loss_val, target_dim=d_t, step=step)\n\n        log_entry = {\n            "step": step,\n            "loss": total_loss.item(),\n            "sim_loss": loss_dict["sim_loss"].item(),\n            "var_loss": loss_dict["var_loss"].item(),\n            "cov_loss": loss_dict["cov_loss"].item(),\n            "ccr_smooth_loss": loss_dict.get("ccr_smooth_loss", 0.0),\n            "ccr_spatial_loss": loss_dict.get("ccr_spatial_loss", 0.0),\n            "per_dim_std": None,\n        }\n\n        # Per-dimension std logging every 500 steps\n        if step % 500 == 0:\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                per_dim_std_train = z_target_dyn[:, :d_t].std(dim=0).cpu().numpy()\n            log_entry["per_dim_std"] = per_dim_std_train.tolist()\n\n        logs.append(log_entry)\n\n        if step % 1000 == 0 or step == total_steps:\n            print(f"  [{name}] seed={seed} step={step:5d}/{total_steps}  "\n                  f"loss={log_entry[\'loss\']:.4f}  sim={log_entry[\'sim_loss\']:.4f}  "\n                  f"var={log_entry[\'var_loss\']:.4f}  cov={log_entry[\'cov_loss\']:.4f}  "\n                  f"ccr_s={log_entry[\'ccr_smooth_loss\']:.4f}  ccr_sp={log_entry[\'ccr_spatial_loss\']:.4f}")\n\n    # Final evaluation at step 8000 (or total_steps for dry_run)\n    print(f"  [{name}] seed={seed} -> final evaluation at step {total_steps}")\n    eval_res = evaluate_run(model, arm_config, seed, device, eval_steps=eval_steps)\n\n    # Extract final training log values\n    final_log = logs[-1]\n    eval_res["final_train_loss"] = final_log["loss"]\n    eval_res["final_sim_loss"] = final_log["sim_loss"]\n    eval_res["final_var_loss"] = final_log["var_loss"]\n    eval_res["final_cov_loss"] = final_log["cov_loss"]\n\n    # Train collapse check: last per_dim_std log at step 8000 (or closest)\n    per_dim_std_train_last = None\n    for entry in reversed(logs):\n        if entry["per_dim_std"] is not None:\n            per_dim_std_train_last = np.array(entry["per_dim_std"])\n            break\n\n    if per_dim_std_train_last is not None:\n        collapsed_train = np.any(per_dim_std_train_last < 0.5)\n        eval_res["collapsed_train"] = bool(collapsed_train)\n        eval_res["per_dim_std_train"] = per_dim_std_train_last.tolist()\n    else:\n        eval_res["collapsed_train"] = False\n        eval_res["per_dim_std_train"] = None\n\n    # Dual collapse criterion\n    eval_res["collapsed"] = eval_res["collapsed_eval"] or eval_res["collapsed_train"]\n\n    # Sanity disqualification: loss > 50\n    if eval_res["final_train_loss"] > 50.0:\n        eval_res["disqualified"] = True\n        eval_res["collapsed"] = True  # Count as collapsed\n    else:\n        eval_res["disqualified"] = False\n\n    return eval_res, model, logs\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Arms configuration\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nARMS = [\n    {\n        "name": "A0 (canonical repeat)",\n        "lr": 3e-4, "var_weight": 25.0, "cov_weight": 25.0,\n        "sim_weight": 25.0, "batch_size": 32,\n        "d_max": 8, "d_t": 3, "dyn_readout": "centroid_gated", "pos_encoding": "none",\n        "ccr_mode": "covariance", "ccr_smooth_weight": 10.0, "ccr_spatial_weight": 10.0,\n        "replay_buffer_capacity": 4000,\n    },\n    {\n        "name": "A1 (batch_size=64)",\n        "lr": 3e-4, "var_weight": 25.0, "cov_weight": 25.0,\n        "sim_weight": 25.0, "batch_size": 64,\n        "d_max": 8, "d_t": 3, "dyn_readout": "centroid_gated", "pos_encoding": "none",\n        "ccr_mode": "covariance", "ccr_smooth_weight": 10.0, "ccr_spatial_weight": 10.0,\n        "replay_buffer_capacity": 4000,\n    },\n    {\n        "name": "A2 (var_weight=50)",\n        "lr": 3e-4, "var_weight": 50.0, "cov_weight": 25.0,\n        "sim_weight": 25.0, "batch_size": 32,\n        "d_max": 8, "d_t": 3, "dyn_readout": "centroid_gated", "pos_encoding": "none",\n        "ccr_mode": "covariance", "ccr_smooth_weight": 10.0, "ccr_spatial_weight": 10.0,\n        "replay_buffer_capacity": 4000,\n    },\n    {\n        "name": "A3 (sim_weight warm-up)",\n        "lr": 3e-4, "var_weight": 25.0, "cov_weight": 25.0,\n        "sim_weight": 25.0, "batch_size": 32,\n        "d_max": 8, "d_t": 3, "dyn_readout": "centroid_gated", "pos_encoding": "none",\n        "ccr_mode": "covariance", "ccr_smooth_weight": 10.0, "ccr_spatial_weight": 10.0,\n        "replay_buffer_capacity": 4000,\n        "sim_weight_ramp": True, "sim_weight_ramp_steps": 1000,\n    },\n    {\n        "name": "A4 (lr=1e-4)",\n        "lr": 1e-4, "var_weight": 25.0, "cov_weight": 25.0,\n        "sim_weight": 25.0, "batch_size": 32,\n        "d_max": 8, "d_t": 3, "dyn_readout": "centroid_gated", "pos_encoding": "none",\n        "ccr_mode": "covariance", "ccr_smooth_weight": 10.0, "ccr_spatial_weight": 10.0,\n        "replay_buffer_capacity": 4000,\n    },\n]\n\nSEEDS = [7, 17, 31, 53, 71, 83, 97, 113, 127, 149]\n\n\ndef _sanitize_arm_name(name):\n    return name.lower().replace(\' \', \'_\').replace(\'(\', \'\').replace(\')\', \'\').replace(\'+\', \'\').replace(\'=\', \'_\')\n\n\ndef _flatten_result(res, runs_dir):\n    flat = {k: v for k, v in res.items() if k not in (\n        "centroid_mse_per_object", "r2_per_dim", "dim_to_obj",\n    )}\n    flat["per_dim_std"] = str(res.get("per_dim_std", []))\n    flat["per_dim_std_train"] = str(res.get("per_dim_std_train", []))\n\n    safe_name = _sanitize_arm_name(res["arm"])\n    run_id = f"{safe_name}_seed{res[\'seed\']}"\n    csv_path = os.path.join(runs_dir, f"{run_id}.csv")\n    json_path = os.path.join(runs_dir, f"{run_id}.json")\n\n    df_row = pd.DataFrame([flat])\n    df_row.to_csv(csv_path, index=False)\n\n    with open(json_path, "w") as f:\n        json.dump(res, f, indent=2, default=str)\n\n    return csv_path\n\n\ndef _run_single_worker(args_tuple):\n    arm, seed, device_str, dry_run, runs_dir, checkpoints_dir = args_tuple\n    device = torch.device(device_str)\n    torch.set_num_threads(1)\n\n    name = arm["name"]\n    print(f"[{name}] seed={seed} -> starting on {device} (dry_run={dry_run})")\n\n    eval_res, model, logs = run_single(arm, seed, device, dry_run=dry_run)\n\n    csv_path = _flatten_result(eval_res, runs_dir)\n    safe_name = _sanitize_arm_name(eval_res["arm"])\n    run_id = f"{safe_name}_seed{eval_res[\'seed\']}"\n    json_path = os.path.join(runs_dir, f"{run_id}.json")\n    print(f"  -> Saved {run_id}  ({csv_path}, {json_path})")\n\n    ckpt_path = os.path.join(checkpoints_dir, f"{safe_name}_seed{seed}.pt")\n    torch.save(model.state_dict(), ckpt_path)\n    print(f"  -> Saved checkpoint {ckpt_path}")\n\n    logs_path = os.path.join(runs_dir, f"{safe_name}_seed{seed}_logs.csv")\n    logs_df = pd.DataFrame(logs)\n    logs_df.to_csv(logs_path, index=False)\n    print(f"  -> Saved training logs {logs_path}")\n\n    return eval_res\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Main\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef main():\n    cpu_count = os.cpu_count() or 2\n    default_workers = min(cpu_count - 1, 8)\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description="Iter_026 Collapse-Rate Sweep"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true",\n                        help="Run 5 training steps per seed for quick correctness check.")\n    parser.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, nargs="+", default=None,\n                        help="Override seed list (default: 10 seeds).")\n    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=default_workers,\n                        help=f"Number of parallel workers (default: min(cpu_count-1, 8)={default_workers}).")\n    parser.add_argument("--sequential", action="store_true",\n                        help="Run sequentially (no parallelism).")\n    parser.add_argument("--device", type=str, default=None,\n                        help="Force device: \'cpu\' or \'cuda\'. Default: auto-detect.")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    dry_run = args.dry_run\n    seeds = args.seeds if args.seeds is not None else SEEDS\n    max_workers = args.workers\n    sequential = args.sequential\n\n    if args.device is not None:\n        device = torch.device(args.device)\n    else:\n        device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")\n    device_str = str(device)\n\n    print(f"CPU count: {cpu_count}")\n    print(f"Using device: {device}")\n    print(f"CUDA available: {torch.cuda.is_available()}")\n    print(f"Dry-run mode: {dry_run}")\n    print(f"Seeds: {seeds}")\n    print(f"Arms: {[a[\'name\'] for a in ARMS]}")\n    if not sequential:\n        print(f"Parallel workers: {max_workers}")\n    print()\n\n    results_dir = "archive/iter_026/results"\n    runs_dir = os.path.join(results_dir, "runs")\n    checkpoints_dir = os.path.join(results_dir, "checkpoints")\n    os.makedirs(runs_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    os.makedirs(checkpoints_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    # Main experiment tasks\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    tasks = []\n    task_labels = []\n    for arm in ARMS:\n        name = arm["name"]\n        for seed in seeds:\n            tasks.append((arm, seed, device_str, dry_run, runs_dir, checkpoints_dir))\n            task_labels.append(f"{name} seed={seed}")\n\n    total_tasks = len(tasks)\n    print(f"Total main tasks to run: {total_tasks}")\n    for i, label in enumerate(task_labels, 1):\n        print(f"  [{i:2d}] {label}")\n    print()\n\n    all_results = []\n\n    if sequential:\n        for i, task in enumerate(tasks):\n            arm, seed = task[0], task[1]\n            print(f"\\n{\'=\'*70}")\n            print(f"TASK [{i+1}/{total_tasks}]: {arm[\'name\']} seed={seed}")\n            print(f"{\'=\'*70}")\n            res = _run_single_worker(task)\n            all_results.append(res)\n    else:\n        with concurrent.futures.ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers) as executor:\n            future_to_label = {}\n            for i, task in enumerate(tasks):\n                future = executor.submit(_run_single_worker, task)\n                future_to_label[future] = task_labels[i]\n\n            done_count = 0\n            for future in concurrent.futures.as_completed(future_to_label):\n                label = future_to_label[future]\n                done_count += 1\n                try:\n                    res = future.result()\n                    all_results.append(res)\n                    print(f"\\n[{done_count}/{total_tasks}] COMPLETED: {label}")\n                except Exception as exc:\n                    print(f"\\n[{done_count}/{total_tasks}] FAILED: {label} -> {exc}")\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    # Aggregate results\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    df_all = pd.DataFrame(all_results)\n\n    numeric_cols = [\n        "collapsed_eval", "collapsed_train", "collapsed", "disqualified",\n        "final_train_loss", "final_sim_loss", "final_var_loss", "final_cov_loss",\n        "centroid_mse_mean", "centroid_r_mean",\n        "vicreg_mean_abs_corr",\n        "delta_r2_color", "r2_dyn_color", "r2_coord_color",\n        "r2_dyn_pos", "r2_coord_pos",\n        "r2_dyn_identity", "r2_coord_identity", "delta_r2_identity",\n    ]\n    for col in numeric_cols:\n        if col in df_all.columns:\n            df_all[col] = pd.to_numeric(df_all[col], errors="coerce")\n\n    summary_path = os.path.join(results_dir, "summary_iter_026.csv")\n    df_all.to_csv(summary_path, index=False)\n    print(f"\\nSaved full results to {summary_path}")\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    # Analysis and report\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #\n    _generate_analysis(df_all, results_dir)\n    print("\\nDone.")\n\n\ndef _generate_analysis(df_all, results_dir):\n    """Generate final_analysis.md with per-arm statistics and pre-registered interpretations."""\n    lines = []\n    lines.append("# Iter_026 Collapse-Rate Sweep Analysis\\n")\n    lines.append(f"**Date:** Auto-generated\\n")\n    lines.append("**Dual collapse criterion:** collapsed = collapsed_eval OR collapsed_train\\n")\n    lines.append("**Sanity disqualification:** final_train_loss > 50 OR per_dim_std_train < 0.5\\n")\n    lines.append("**Arms:** A0 (canonical), A1 (batch=64), A2 (var=50), A3 (sim warm-up), A4 (lr=1e-4)\\n")\n    lines.append("---\\n")\n\n    def _fmt(val, fmt=".4f"):\n        if val is None or (isinstance(val, float) and np.isnan(val)):\n            return "N/A"\n        return f"{val:{fmt}}"\n\n    # Per-arm summary\n    lines.append("## Per-Arm Summary (Step = 8000)\\n")\n    for arm_name in [a["name"] for a in ARMS]:\n        df_arm = df_all[df_all["arm"] == arm_name]\n        if len(df_arm) == 0:\n            continue\n        lines.append(f"### {arm_name}\\n")\n        lines.append(f"- N seeds: {len(df_arm)}\\n")\n\n        # Dual criterion\n        collapse_rate_dual = df_arm["collapsed"].mean()\n        collapsed_count_dual = int(df_arm["collapsed"].sum())\n        lines.append(f"- Collapse rate (dual criterion): {collapse_rate_dual:.2f} ({collapsed_count_dual}/{len(df_arm)})\\n")\n\n        # Eval-only\n        collapse_rate_eval = df_arm["collapsed_eval"].mean()\n        collapsed_count_eval = int(df_arm["collapsed_eval"].sum())\n        lines.append(f"- Collapse rate (eval-only): {collapse_rate_eval:.2f} ({collapsed_count_eval}/{len(df_arm)})\\n")\n\n        # Train-only\n        collapse_rate_train = df_arm["collapsed_train"].mean()\n        collapsed_count_train = int(df_arm["collapsed_train"].sum())\n        lines.append(f"- Collapse rate (train-only): {collapse_rate_train:.2f} ({collapsed_count_train}/{len(df_arm)})\\n")\n\n        # Disqualified\n        disqualified_count = int(df_arm["disqualified"].sum())\n        if disqualified_count > 0:\n            lines.append(f"- **Disqualified seeds (loss > 50):** {disqualified_count}\\n")\n\n        lines.append(f"- Mean final train loss: {_fmt(df_arm[\'final_train_loss\'].mean(), \'.4f\')} +/- {_fmt(df_arm[\'final_train_loss\'].std(), \'.4f\')}\\n")\n        lines.append(f"- Mean final sim loss: {_fmt(df_arm[\'final_sim_loss\'].mean(), \'.4f\')}\\n")\n        lines.append(f"- Mean final var loss: {_fmt(df_arm[\'final_var_loss\'].mean(), \'.4f\')}\\n")\n        lines.append(f"- Mean final cov loss: {_fmt(df_arm[\'final_cov_loss\'].mean(), \'.4f\')}\\n")\n        lines.append(f"- Centroid MSE (REF ONLY): {_fmt(df_arm[\'centroid_mse_mean\'].mean(), \'.2f\')}\\n")\n        lines.append(f"- delta_R2_color (REF ONLY): {_fmt(df_arm[\'delta_r2_color\'].mean(), \'.4f\')}\\n")\n        lines.append(f"- Mean abs corr: {_fmt(df_arm[\'vicreg_mean_abs_corr\'].mean(), \'.3f\')}\\n")\n\n        # Per-seed details\n        lines.append("\\n**Per-seed details:**\\n")\n        lines.append("| seed | collapsed_eval | collapsed_train | collapsed | disqualified | final_loss | per_dim_std_eval | per_dim_std_train | centroid_mse | delta_r2_color |\\n")\n        lines.append("|------|----------------|-----------------|-----------|--------------|------------|------------------|-------------------|--------------|----------------|\\n")\n        for _, row in df_arm.iterrows():\n            s = int(row["seed"])\n            ce = "Y" if row["collapsed_eval"] else "N"\n            ct = "Y" if row["collapsed_train"] else "N"\n            c = "Y" if row["collapsed"] else "N"\n            dq = "Y" if row["disqualified"] else "N"\n            fl = _fmt(row.get("final_train_loss"), ".4f")\n            pds = str(row.get("per_dim_std", []))\n            pds_t = str(row.get("per_dim_std_train", []))\n            cm = _fmt(row.get("centroid_mse_mean"), ".2f")\n            dr = _fmt(row.get("delta_r2_color"), ".4f")\n            lines.append(f"| {s} | {ce} | {ct} | {c} | {dq} | {fl} | {pds} | {pds_t} | {cm} | {dr} |\\n")\n        lines.append("\\n")\n\n    # Gate check\n    lines.append("## Gate Check\\n\\n")\n    gate_cleared = False\n    cleared_arms = []\n    for arm_name in [a["name"] for a in ARMS]:\n        df_arm = df_all[df_all["arm"] == arm_name]\n        if len(df_arm) == 0:\n            continue\n        collapse_rate = df_arm["collapsed"].mean()\n        if collapse_rate <= 0.10:\n            gate_cleared = True\n            cleared_arms.append(arm_name)\n            lines.append(f"- **{arm_name}:** PASS (collapse rate {collapse_rate:.2f} <= 10%)\\n")\n        else:\n            lines.append(f"- **{arm_name}:** FAIL (collapse rate {collapse_rate:.2f} > 10%)\\n")\n\n    lines.append("\\n")\n    if gate_cleared:\n        lines.append(f"**Gate cleared by:** {\', \'.join(cleared_arms)}\\n")\n        lines.append("All arms completed their full 10-seed runs as pre-registered.\\n")\n    else:\n        lines.append("**Measured null: no swept configuration cleared the <=10% gate under the pre-registered protocol.**\\n")\n        lines.append("All arms completed their full 10-seed runs as pre-registered.\\n")\n    lines.append("\\n")\n\n    # Sanity check\n    lines.append("## Sanity Check\\n\\n")\n    total_disqualified = int(df_all["disqualified"].sum())\n    if total_disqualified > 0:\n        lines.append(f"- **WARNING:** {total_disqualified} seed(s) disqualified due to final_train_loss > 50.\\n")\n        dq_df = df_all[df_all["disqualified"] == True]\n        for _, row in dq_df.iterrows():\n            lines.append(f"  - {row[\'arm\']} seed={int(row[\'seed\'])}: loss={row[\'final_train_loss\']:.2f}\\n")\n    else:\n        lines.append("- No seeds disqualified. All final losses <= 50.\\n")\n    lines.append("\\n")\n\n    # Reference-only downstream metrics disclaimer\n    lines.append("## Reference-Only Downstream Metrics (NOT used for regime selection)\\n\\n")\n    lines.append("The following metrics are recorded for diagnostic purposes only. archive\iter_011\iter_001\stdout.txt:145: [Tool Call: write_file({'content': 'import numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\nimport collections\n\ndef calculate_centroid_and_variance(a_spatial):\n    """\n    Computes spatial centroid and soft spatial variance for each channel.\n    Args:\n        a_spatial (Tensor): shape (B, d_max, 128)\n    Returns:\n        x_mean (Tensor): shape (B, d_max)\n        var (Tensor): shape (B, d_max)\n    """\n    p_c = F.softmax(a_spatial, dim=-1) # shape (B, d_max, 128)\n    coords = torch.arange(128, device=a_spatial.device, dtype=torch.float32)\n    x_mean = torch.sum(coords * p_c, dim=-1) # shape (B, d_max)\n    var = torch.sum(((coords.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(1) - x_mean.unsqueeze(-1)) ** 2) * p_c, dim=-1) # shape (B, d_max)\n    return x_mean, var\n\nclass DualStreamEncoder(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, d_max=8):\n        """\n        1D CNN mapping input of shape (B, 3, 128) to decoupled coordinate and dynamics streams.\n        """\n        super().__init__()\n        self.d_max = d_max\n        self.conv1 = nn.Conv1d(3, 16, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        self.conv2 = nn.Conv1d(16, 32, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        self.conv3 = nn.Conv1d(32, 64, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        self.conv4 = nn.Conv1d(64, 128, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        \n        # Dual stream heads\n        self.conv_spatial_coord = nn.Conv1d(128, d_max, kernel_size=1)\n        self.conv_spatial_dyn = nn.Conv1d(128, d_max, kernel_size=1)\n        \n    def forward_spatial(self, x):\n        """\n        Returns spatial feature map of shape (B, d_max, 128) for the coordinate stream.\n        To ensure backprop through loss_spatial only flows to conv_spatial_coord,\n        we detach the output of conv4 before passing to conv_spatial_coord.\n        """\n        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv2(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv3(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv4(x)) # (B, 128, 8)\n        \n        # Detach before conv_spatial_coord to stop gradients flowing to conv1-4\n        x_coord = self.conv_spatial_coord(x.detach())  # (B, d_max, 8)\n        x_coord = F.interpolate(x_coord, size=128, mode=\'linear\', align_corners=False) # (B, d_max, 128)\n        return x_coord\n\n    def forward_dynamics(self, x):\n        """\n        Returns the dynamics representation of shape (B, d_max).\n        Gradients on this stream flow back to conv_spatial_dyn and conv1-4.\n        """\n        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv2(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv3(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv4(x)) # (B, 128, 8)\n        \n        x_dyn = self.conv_spatial_dyn(x) # (B, d_max, 8)\n        return x_dyn.mean(dim=-1) # (B, d_max)\n\n    def forward(self, x):\n        """\n        Returns both streams:\n        z_coord: soft centroids, shape (B, d_max)\n        z_dyn: dynamics representations, shape (B, d_max)\n        """\n        a_spatial = self.forward_spatial(x)\n        z_coord, _ = calculate_centroid_and_variance(a_spatial)\n        z_dyn = self.forward_dynamics(x)\n        return z_coord, z_dyn\n\nclass DualStreamPredictor(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, d_max=8, h=3):\n        """\n        MLP forecasting target z_{t+1} of size (B, 2 * D_max)\n        from history of active latent states (B, H * 2 * D_max).\n        """\n        super().__init__()\n        self.d_max = d_max\n        self.h = h\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.Linear(h * 2 * d_max, 128),\n            nn.ReLU(),\n            nn.Linear(128, 128),\n            nn.ReLU(),\n            nn.Linear(128, 2 * d_max)\n        )\n        \n    def forward(self, z_coord_history, z_dyn_history, d_t, mask_coord=False):\n        """\n        Args:\n            z_coord_history (Tensor): Shape (B, H, D_max)\n            z_dyn_history (Tensor): Shape (B, H, D_max)\n            d_t (int): Current active latent dimension.\n            mask_coord (bool): If True, zero out the coordinate input.\n        """\n        # Option to zero out coordinate input completely\n        if mask_coord:\n            z_coord_history = torch.zeros_like(z_coord_history)\n            \n        # Zero out inactive dimensions (index >= d_t)\n        mask_c = torch.zeros_like(z_coord_history)\n        mask_c[:, :, :d_t] = 1.0\n        z_coord_active = z_coord_history * mask_c\n        \n        mask_d = torch.zeros_like(z_dyn_history)\n        mask_d[:, :, :d_t] = 1.0\n        z_dyn_active = z_dyn_history * mask_d\n        \n        # Concatenate coordinate and dynamics histories along feature dimension: (B, H, 2 * d_max)\n        z_history = torch.cat([z_coord_active, z_dyn_active], dim=-1)\n        \n        # Flatten history: (B, H * 2 * d_max)\n        z_history_flat = z_history.reshape(-1, self.h * 2 * self.d_max)\n        \n        # Predict both streams\n        pred = self.net(z_history_flat) # (B, 2 * d_max)\n        \n        # Split into coord and dyn predictions\n        pred_coord, pred_dyn = torch.split(pred, self.d_max, dim=-1)\n        \n        # Zero out inactive dimensions in the outputs\n        out_mask = torch.zeros_like(pred_coord)\n        out_mask[:, :d_t] = 1.0\n        \n        pred_coord_active = pred_coord * out_mask\n        pred_dyn_active = pred_dyn * out_mask\n        \n        return pred_coord_active, pred_dyn_active\n\nclass DualStreamJEPASpatial(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, d_max=8, h=3, k=4, cooldown=300, stabilization_period=100):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.d_max = d_max\n        self.h = h\n        self.k = k\n        self.cooldown = cooldown\n        self.stabilization_period = stabilization_period\n        \n        self.encoder = DualStreamEncoder(d_max=d_max)\n        self.predictor = DualStreamPredictor(d_max=d_max, h=h)\n        \n        # Dynamic state tracking\n        self.d_t = 2\n        self.steps_since_recruitment = cooldown  # start outside of cooldown\n        self.error_buffer = collections.deque(maxlen=500)  # collects EMA of error during stable periods\n        self.ema_error = None\n        self.ema_alpha = 0.05\n\n    def reset_error_buffer(self):\n        self.error_buffer.clear()\n        self.ema_error = None\n\n    def calculate_centroid_and_variance(self, a_spatial):\n        return calculate_centroid_and_variance(a_spatial)\n\n    def forward(self, x_hist, x_target, sim_weight=25.0, var_weight=25.0, cov_weight=25.0, lambda_spatial=0.0, k_chan=None, mask_coord=False):\n        """\n        Args:\n            x_hist (Tensor): shape (B, H, 3, 128)\n            x_target (Tensor): shape (B, 3, 128)\n            sim_weight (float): VICReg invariance loss weight\n            var_weight (float): VICReg variance loss weight\n            cov_weight (float): VICReg covariance loss weight\n            lambda_spatial (float): spatial bottleneck loss weight\n            k_chan (int, optional): channel index to minimize spatial variance on. Defaults to d_t - 1.\n            mask_coord (bool): whether to zero out coordinate history in predictor.\n        """\n        B, H, C, W = x_hist.shape\n        x_hist_flat = x_hist.reshape(B * H, C, W)\n        \n        # Encode history\n        z_hist_coord_flat, z_hist_dyn_flat = self.encoder(x_hist_flat)\n        z_hist_coord = z_hist_coord_flat.reshape(B, H, self.d_max)\n        z_hist_dyn = z_hist_dyn_flat.reshape(B, H, self.d_max)\n        \n        # Encode target\n        z_target_coord, z_target_dyn = self.encoder(x_target)\n        \n        # Apply stabilization stop-gradient logic to history and target representations\n        if self.steps_since_recruitment < self.stabilization_period and self.d_t > 1:\n            z_hist_coord_stable = torch.cat([\n                z_hist_coord[:, :, :self.d_t-1].detach(),\n                z_hist_coord[:, :, self.d_t-1:]\n            ], dim=-1)\n            z_hist_dyn_stable = torch.cat([\n                z_hist_dyn[:, :, :self.d_t-1].detach(),\n                z_hist_dyn[:, :, self.d_t-1:]\n            ], dim=-1)\n            \n            z_target_coord_stable = torch.cat([\n                z_target_coord[:, :self.d_t-1].detach(),\n                z_target_coord[:, self.d_t-1:]\n            ], dim=-1)\n            z_target_dyn_stable = torch.cat([\n                z_target_dyn[:, :self.d_t-1].detach(),\n                z_target_dyn[:, self.d_t-1:]\n            ], dim=-1)\n        else:\n            z_hist_coord_stable = z_hist_coord\n            z_hist_dyn_stable = z_hist_dyn\n            \n            z_target_coord_stable = z_target_coord\n            z_target_dyn_stable = z_target_dyn\n            \n        # Predictive Coupling & Stop-Gradients on coordinate stream\n        # This prevents prediction gradients from flowing to the coordinate stream representation\n        z_hist_coord_pred = z_hist_coord_stable.detach()\n        z_target_coord_pred = z_target_coord_stable.detach()\n        \n        # Predict target representations\n        z_pred_coord, z_pred_dyn = self.predictor(\n            z_hist_coord_pred, \n            z_hist_dyn_stable, \n            self.d_t, \n            mask_coord=mask_coord\n        )\n        \n        # Apply stabilization stop-gradient to predictor outputs\n        if self.steps_since_recruitment < self.stabilization_period and self.d_t > 1:\n            z_pred_coord_stable = torch.cat([\n                z_pred_coord[:, :self.d_t-1].detach(),\n                z_pred_coord[:, self.d_t-1:]\n            ], dim=-1)\n            z_pred_dyn_stable = torch.cat([\n                z_pred_dyn[:, :self.d_t-1].detach(),\n                z_pred_dyn[:, self.d_t-1:]\n            ], dim=-1)\n        else:\n            z_pred_coord_stable = z_pred_coord\n            z_pred_dyn_stable = z_pred_dyn\n            \n        # Select active dimensions\n        z_pred_coord_active = z_pred_coord_stable[:, :self.d_t]\n        z_target_coord_active = z_target_coord_stable[:, :self.d_t]\n        \n        z_pred_dyn_active = z_pred_dyn_stable[:, :self.d_t]\n        z_target_dyn_active = z_target_dyn_stable[:, :self.d_t]\n        \n        # 1. Similarity (Invariance) Loss\n        sim_loss_coord = F.mse_loss(z_pred_coord_active, z_target_coord_pred[:, :self.d_t])\n        sim_loss_dyn = F.mse_loss(z_pred_dyn_active, z_target_dyn_active)\n        sim_loss = sim_loss_coord + sim_loss_dyn\n        \n        # 2. Variance Loss\n        def calc_var_loss(x, gamma=1.0, eps=1e-4):\n            mean = x.mean(dim=0)\n            var = torch.mean((x - mean)**2, dim=0)\n            std = torch.sqrt(var + eps)\n            return torch.mean(F.relu(gamma - std))\n            \n        var_loss_coord = 0.5 * (calc_var_loss(z_pred_coord_active) + calc_var_loss(z_target_coord_active))\n        var_loss_dyn = 0.5 * (calc_var_loss(z_pred_dyn_active) + calc_var_loss(z_target_dyn_active))\n        var_loss = var_loss_coord + var_loss_dyn\n        \n        # 3. Covariance Loss\n        def calc_cov_loss(x):\n            B, d = x.shape\n            if B <= 1 or d <= 1:\n                return torch.tensor(0.0, device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)\n            mean = x.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)\n            x_centered = x - mean\n            cov = (x_centered.T @ x_centered) / (B - 1)\n            diag = torch.diagonal(cov)\n            off_diag = cov - torch.diag(diag)\n            return (off_diag ** 2).sum() / d\n            \n        cov_loss_coord = 0.5 * (calc_cov_loss(z_pred_coord_active) + calc_cov_loss(z_target_coord_active))\n        cov_loss_dyn = 0.5 * (calc_cov_loss(z_pred_dyn_active) + calc_cov_loss(z_target_dyn_active))\n        cov_loss = cov_loss_coord + cov_loss_dyn\n        \n        base_loss = sim_weight * sim_loss + var_weight * var_loss + cov_weight * cov_loss\n        \n        # 4. Spatial Bottleneck Loss\n        if lambda_spatial > 0:\n            z_target_spatial = self.encoder.forward_spatial(x_target)\n            _, var_all = self.calculate_centroid_and_variance(z_target_spatial)\n            if k_chan is None:\n                k_chan = self.d_t - 1\n            var_k = var_all[:, k_chan]\n            loss_spatial = lambda_spatial * var_k.mean()\n            loss = base_loss + loss_spatial\n        else:\n            loss_spatial = torch.tensor(0.0, device=x_target.device, dtype=x_target.dtype)\n            loss = base_loss\n            \n        return {\n            "loss": loss,\n            "sim_loss": sim_loss,\n            "sim_loss_coord": sim_loss_coord,\n            "sim_loss_dyn": sim_loss_dyn,\n            "var_loss": var_loss,\n            "var_loss_coord": var_loss_coord,\n            "var_loss_dyn": var_loss_dyn,\n            "cov_loss": cov_loss,\n            "cov_loss_coord": cov_loss_coord,\n            "cov_loss_dyn": cov_loss_dyn,\n            "loss_spatial": loss_spatial\n        }, (z_pred_coord, z_pred_dyn), (z_target_coord, z_target_dyn)\n\n    def update_recruitment_logic(self, error_val, target_dim=None):\n        if target_dim is None:\n            target_dim = self.d_t\n\n        if self.ema_error is None:\n            self.ema_error = error_val\n        else:\n            self.ema_error = self.ema_alpha * error_val + (1.0 - self.ema_alpha) * self.ema_error\n            \n        self.steps_since_recruitment += 1\n        \n        if self.d_t == target_dim:\n            self.error_buffer.append(self.ema_error)\n            \n        if self.d_t == target_dim and self.steps_since_recruitment > self.cooldown:\n            if len(self.error_buffer) >= 200:\n                mean = np.mean(self.error_buffer)\n                std = np.std(self.error_buffer)\n                if self.ema_error > mean + self.k * std:\n                    self.d_t = target_dim + 1\n                    self.steps_since_recruitment = 0\n                    print(f"[GDASR] Recruited dimension! d_t increased to {self.d_t} at error {self.ema_error:.4f} (baseline mean={mean:.4f}, std={std:.4f})")\n\n    def clone(self):\n        """\n        Self-cloning capability: Returns a copy of this model with identical parameters and dynamic state.\n        """\n        import copy\n        cloned = DualStreamJEPASpatial(\n            d_max=self.d_max,\n            h=self.h,\n            k=self.k,\n            cooldown=self.cooldown,\n            stabilization_period=self.stabilization_period\n        )\n        cloned.d_t = self.d_t\n        cloned.load_state_dict(self.state_dict())\n        cloned.steps_since_recruitment = self.steps_since_recruitment\n        cloned.error_buffer = copy.deepcopy(self.error_buffer)\n        cloned.ema_error = self.ema_error\n        return cloned\n\n\nclass PDRCEncoder(DualStreamEncoder):\n    def __init__(self, d_max=8):\n        super().__init__(d_max=d_max)\n        self.stage = 1\n\n    def forward_spatial(self, x):\n        """\n        Returns spatial feature map of shape (B, d_max, 128) for the coordinate stream.\n        To ensure backprop through loss_spatial only flows to conv_spatial_coord,\n        we detach the output of conv4 before passing to conv_spatial_coord in Stage 2.\n        In Stage 1, we allow gradients to flow back to conv1-4.\n        """\n        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv2(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv3(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv4(x)) # (B, 128, 8)\n        \n        if self.stage == 2:\n            x_coord = self.conv_spatial_coord(x.detach())\n        else:\n            x_coord = self.conv_spatial_coord(x)\n            \n        x_coord = F.interpolate(x_coord, size=128, mode=\'linear\', align_corners=False) # (B, d_max, 128)\n        return x_coord\n\n\nclass PDRCJEPASpatial(DualStreamJEPASpatial):\n    def __init__(self, d_max=8, h=3, k=4, cooldown=300, stabilization_period=100, stage=1):\n        super().__init__(d_max=d_max, h=h, k=k, cooldown=cooldown, stabilization_period=stabilization_period)\n        self.encoder = PDRCEncoder(d_max=d_max)\n        self._stage = stage\n        self.stage = stage # Trigger property setter to configure requires_grad\n\n    @property\n    def stage(self):\n        return self._stage\n\n    @stage.setter\n    def stage(self, value):\n        if value not in [1, 2]:\n            raise ValueError("Stage must be 1 or 2.")\n        self._stage = value\n        self.encoder.stage = value\n        \n        # Stage 1: All parameters should have requires_grad=True\n        # Stage 2: Set requires_grad=False on encoder.conv_spatial_coord parameters\n        #          The dynamics stream and predictor should remain fully trainable\n        if value == 1:\n            for p in self.parameters():\n                p.requires_grad = True\n        elif value == 2:\n            for name, p in self.named_parameters():\n                if "encoder.conv_spatial_coord" in name:\n                    p.requires_grad = False\n                else:\n                    p.requires_grad = True\n\n    def forward(self, x_hist, x_target, sim_weight=25.0, var_weight=25.0, cov_weight=25.0, lambda_spatial=0.0, k_chan=None, mask_coord=False):\n        B, H, C, W = x_hist.shape\n        x_hist_flat = x_hist.reshape(B * H, C, W)\n        \n        # Encode history\n        z_hist_coord_flat, z_hist_dyn_flat = self.encoder(x_hist_flat)\n        z_hist_coord = z_hist_coord_flat.reshape(B, H, self.d_max)\n        z_hist_dyn = z_hist_dyn_flat.reshape(B, H, self.d_max)\n        \n        # Encode target\n        z_target_coord, z_target_dyn = self.encoder(x_target)\n        \n        # Stabilization stop-gradient logic depending on stage\n        if self.stage == 1:\n            # Stage 1: No detaching at all on coordinate representations\n            z_hist_coord_stable = z_hist_coord\n            z_target_coord_stable = z_target_coord\n        else:\n            # Stage 2: Apply standard stabilization stop-gradients to coordinate stream\n            if self.steps_since_recruitment < self.stabilization_period and self.d_t > 1:\n                z_hist_coord_stable = torch.cat([\n                    z_hist_coord[:, :, :self.d_t-1].detach(),\n                    z_hist_coord[:, :, self.d_t-1:]\n                ], dim=-1)\n                z_target_coord_stable = torch.cat([\n                    z_target_coord[:, :self.d_t-1].detach(),\n                    z_target_coord[:, self.d_t-1:]\n                ], dim=-1)\n            else:\n                z_hist_coord_stable = z_hist_coord\n                z_target_coord_stable = z_target_coord\n\n        # Dynamics stream always has stabilization stop-gradient logic\n        if self.steps_since_recruitment < self.stabilization_period and self.d_t > 1:\n            z_hist_dyn_stable = torch.cat([\n                z_hist_dyn[:, :, :self.d_t-1].detach(),\n                z_hist_dyn[:, :, self.d_t-1:]\n            ], dim=-1)\n            z_target_dyn_stable = torch.cat([\n                z_target_dyn[:, :self.d_t-1].detach(),\n                z_target_dyn[:, self.d_t-1:]\n            ], dim=-1)\n        else:\n            z_hist_dyn_stable = z_hist_dyn\n            z_target_dyn_stable = z_target_dyn\n\n        # Predictive Coupling & Stop-Gradients on coordinate stream\n        if self.stage == 1:\n            # Stage 1: Do NOT detach before passing to predictor or in similarity loss\n            z_hist_coord_pred = z_hist_coord_stable\n            z_target_coord_pred = z_target_coord_stable\n        else:\n            # Stage 2: Detach z_hist_coord and z_target_coord before predictor or similarity loss\n            z_hist_coord_pred = z_hist_coord_stable.detach()\n            z_target_coord_pred = z_target_coord_stable.detach()\n\n        # Predict target representations\n        z_pred_coord, z_pred_dyn = self.predictor(\n            z_hist_coord_pred, \n            z_hist_dyn_stable, \n            self.d_t, \n            mask_coord=mask_coord\n        )\n\n        # Apply stabilization stop-gradient to predictor outputs\n        if self.stage == 1:\n            # Stage 1: Do NOT detach coordinate predictor output\n            z_pred_coord_stable = z_pred_coord\n        else:\n            if self.steps_since_recruitment < self.stabilization_period and self.d_t > 1:\n                z_pred_coord_stable = torch.cat([\n                    z_pred_coord[:, :self.d_t-1].detach(),\n                    z_pred_coord[:, self.d_t-1:]\n                ], dim=-1)\n            else:\n                z_pred_coord_stable = z_pred_coord\n\n        if self.steps_since_recruitment < self.stabilization_period and self.d_t > 1:\n            z_pred_dyn_stable = torch.cat([\n                z_pred_dyn[:, :self.d_t-1].detach(),\n                z_pred_dyn[:, self.d_t-1:]\n            ], dim=-1)\n        else:\n            z_pred_dyn_stable = z_pred_dyn\n\n        # Select active dimensions\n        z_pred_coord_active = z_pred_coord_stable[:, :self.d_t]\n        z_target_coord_active = z_target_coord_stable[:, :self.d_t]\n        \n        z_pred_dyn_active = z_pred_dyn_stable[:, :self.d_t]\n        z_target_dyn_active = z_target_dyn_stable[:, :self.d_t]\n        \n        # 1. Similarity (Invariance) Loss\n        sim_loss_coord = F.mse_loss(z_pred_coord_active, z_target_coord_pred[:, :self.d_t])\n        sim_loss_dyn = F.mse_loss(z_pred_dyn_active, z_target_dyn_active)\n        sim_loss = sim_loss_coord + sim_loss_dyn\n        \n        # 2. Variance Loss\n        def calc_var_loss(x, gamma=1.0, eps=1e-4):\n            mean = x.mean(dim=0)\n            var = torch.mean((x - mean)**2, dim=0)\n            std = torch.sqrt(var + eps)\n            return torch.mean(F.relu(gamma - std))\n            \n        var_loss_coord = 0.5 * (calc_var_loss(z_pred_coord_active) + calc_var_loss(z_target_coord_active))\n        var_loss_dyn = 0.5 * (calc_var_loss(z_pred_dyn_active) + calc_var_loss(z_target_dyn_active))\n        var_loss = var_loss_coord + var_loss_dyn\n        \n        # 3. Covariance Loss\n        def calc_cov_loss(x):\n            B, d = x.shape\n            if B <= 1 or d <= 1:\n                return torch.tensor(0.0, device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)\n            mean = x.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)\n            x_centered = x - mean\n            cov = (x_centered.T @ x_centered) / (B - 1)\n            diag = torch.diagonal(cov)\n            off_diag = cov - torch.diag(diag)\n            return (off_diag ** 2).sum() / d\n            \n        cov_loss_coord = 0.5 * (calc_cov_loss(z_pred_coord_active) + calc_cov_loss(z_target_coord_active))\n        cov_loss_dyn = 0.5 * (calc_cov_loss(z_pred_dyn_active) + calc_cov_loss(z_target_dyn_active))\n        cov_loss = cov_loss_coord + cov_loss_dyn\n        \n        base_loss = sim_weight * sim_loss + var_weight * var_loss + cov_weight * cov_loss\n        \n        # 4. Spatial Bottleneck Loss\n        if lambda_spatial > 0:\n            z_target_spatial = self.encoder.forward_spatial(x_target)\n            _, var_all = self.calculate_centroid_and_variance(z_target_spatial)\n            if k_chan is None:\n                k_chan = self.d_t - 1\n            var_k = var_all[:, k_chan]\n            loss_spatial = lambda_spatial * var_k.mean()\n            loss = base_loss + loss_spatial\n        else:\n            loss_spatial = torch.tensor(0.0, device=x_target.device, dtype=x_target.dtype)\n            loss = base_loss\n            \n        return {\n            "loss": loss,\n            "sim_loss": sim_loss,\n            "sim_loss_coord": sim_loss_coord,\n            "sim_loss_dyn": sim_loss_dyn,\n            "var_loss": var_loss,\n            "var_loss_coord": var_loss_coord,\n            "var_loss_dyn": var_loss_dyn,\n            "cov_loss": cov_loss,\n            "cov_loss_coord": cov_loss_coord,\n            "cov_loss_dyn": cov_loss_dyn,\n            "loss_spatial": loss_spatial\n        }, (z_pred_coord, z_pred_dyn), (z_target_coord, z_target_dyn)\n\n    def clone(self):\n        import copy\n        cloned = PDRCJEPASpatial(\n            d_max=self.d_max,\n            h=self.h,\n            k=self.k,\n            cooldown=self.cooldown,\n            stabilization_period=self.stabilization_period,\n            stage=self.stage\n        )\n        cloned.d_t = self.d_t\n        cloned.load_state_dict(self.state_dict())\n        cloned.steps_since_recruitment = self.steps_since_recruitment\n        cloned.error_buffer = copy.deepcopy(self.error_buffer)\n        cloned.ema_error = self.ema_error\n        return cloned\n\n\nclass NonParametricEncoder(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, d_max=8):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.d_max = d_max\n        self.conv1 = nn.Conv1d(3, 16, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        self.conv2 = nn.Conv1d(16, 32, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        self.conv3 = nn.Conv1d(32, 64, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        self.conv4 = nn.Conv1d(64, 128, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        self.conv_spatial = nn.Conv1d(128, d_max, kernel_size=1)\n\n    def forward_spatial(self, x):\n        """\n        Returns spatial feature map of shape (B, d_max, 128) for the encoder.\n        """\n        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv2(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv3(x))\n        x = archive\iter_013\iter_003\stdout.txt:191: [Tool Call: write_file({'content': 'import numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\nimport collections\n\ndef calculate_centroid_and_variance(a_spatial):\n    """\n    Computes spatial centroid and soft spatial variance for each channel.\n    Args:\n        a_spatial (Tensor): shape (B, d_max, 128)\n    Returns:\n        x_mean (Tensor): shape (B, d_max)\n        var (Tensor): shape (B, d_max)\n    """\n    p_c = F.softmax(a_spatial, dim=-1) # shape (B, d_max, 128)\n    coords = torch.arange(128, device=a_spatial.device, dtype=torch.float32)\n    x_mean = torch.sum(coords * p_c, dim=-1) # shape (B, d_max)\n    var = torch.sum(((coords.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(1) - x_mean.unsqueeze(-1)) ** 2) * p_c, dim=-1) # shape (B, d_max)\n    return x_mean, var\n\ndef add_positional_encoding(x, pos_encoding="none"):\n    if pos_encoding == "none":\n        return x\n    B, _, W = x.shape\n    device = x.device\n    coords = torch.arange(W, dtype=torch.float32, device=device)\n    if pos_encoding == "linear":\n        pos_linear = coords / max(1.0, float(W - 1))\n        pos_linear = pos_linear.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, 1, -1)\n        return torch.cat([x, pos_linear], dim=1)\n    elif pos_encoding == "sinusoidal":\n        pos_sin_10 = torch.sin(coords / 10.0)\n        pos_cos_10 = torch.cos(coords / 10.0)\n        pos_sin_100 = torch.sin(coords / 100.0)\n        pos_cos_100 = torch.cos(coords / 100.0)\n        pos_embeds = torch.stack([pos_sin_10, pos_cos_10, pos_sin_100, pos_cos_100], dim=0)\n        pos_embeds = pos_embeds.unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1, -1)\n        return torch.cat([x, pos_embeds], dim=1)\n    return x\n\nclass DualStreamEncoder(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, d_max=8):\n        """\n        1D CNN mapping input of shape (B, 3, 128) to decoupled coordinate and dynamics streams.\n        """\n        super().__init__()\n        self.d_max = d_max\n        self.conv1 = nn.Conv1d(3, 16, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        self.conv2 = nn.Conv1d(16, 32, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        self.conv3 = nn.Conv1d(32, 64, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        self.conv4 = nn.Conv1d(64, 128, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        \n        # Dual stream heads\n        self.conv_spatial_coord = nn.Conv1d(128, d_max, kernel_size=1)\n        self.conv_spatial_dyn = nn.Conv1d(128, d_max, kernel_size=1)\n        \n    def forward_spatial(self, x):\n        """\n        Returns spatial feature map of shape (B, d_max, 128) for the coordinate stream.\n        To ensure backprop through loss_spatial only flows to conv_spatial_coord,\n        we detach the output of conv4 before passing to conv_spatial_coord.\n        """\n        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv2(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv3(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv4(x)) # (B, 128, 8)\n        \n        # Detach before conv_spatial_coord to stop gradients flowing to conv1-4\n        x_coord = self.conv_spatial_coord(x.detach())  # (B, d_max, 8)\n        x_coord = F.interpolate(x_coord, size=128, mode=\'linear\', align_corners=False) # (B, d_max, 128)\n        return x_coord\n\n    def forward_dynamics(self, x):\n        """\n        Returns the dynamics representation of shape (B, d_max).\n        Gradients on this stream flow back to conv_spatial_dyn and conv1-4.\n        """\n        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv2(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv3(x))\n        x = F.relu(self.conv4(x)) # (B, 128, 8)\n        \n        x_dyn = self.conv_spatial_dyn(x) # (B, d_max, 8)\n        return x_dyn.mean(dim=-1) # (B, d_max)\n\n    def forward(self, x):\n        """\n        Returns both streams:\n        z_coord: soft centroids, shape (B, d_max)\n        z_dyn: dynamics representations, shape (B, d_max)\n        """\n        a_spatial = self.forward_spatial(x)\n        z_coord, _ = calculate_centroid_and_variance(a_spatial)\n        z_dyn = self.forward_dynamics(x)\n        return z_coord, z_dyn\n\n\nclass DualStreamPredictor(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, d_max=8, h=3):\n        """\n        MLP forecasting target z_{t+1} of size (B, 2 * D_max)\n        from history of active latent states (B, H * 2 * D_max).\n        """\n        super().__init__()\n        self.d_max = d_max\n        self.h = h\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.Linear(h * 2 * d_max, 128),\n            nn.ReLU(),\n            nn.Linear(128, 128),\n            nn.ReLU(),\n            nn.Linear(128, 2 * d_max)\n        )\n        \n    def forward(self, z_coord_history, z_dyn_history, d_t, mask_coord=False):\n        """\n        Args:\n            z_coord_history (Tensor): Shape (B, H, D_max)\n            z_dyn_history (Tensor): Shape (B, H, D_max)\n            d_t (int): Current active latent dimension.\n            mask_coord (bool): If True, zero out the coordinate input.\n        """\n        # Option to zero out coordinate input completely\n        if mask_coord:\n            z_coord_history = torch.zeros_like(z_coord_history)\n            \n        # Zero out inactive dimensions (index >= d_t)\n        mask_c = torch.zeros_like(z_coord_history)\n        mask_c[:, :, :d_t] = 1.0\n        z_coord_active = z_coord_history * mask_c\n        \n        mask_d = torch.zeros_like(z_dyn_history)\n        mask_d[:, :, :d_t] = 1.0\n        z_dyn_active = z_dyn_history * mask_d\n        \n        # Concatenate coordinate and dynamics histories along feature dimension: (B, H, 2 * d_max)\n        z_history = torch.cat([z_coord_active, z_dyn_active], dim=-1)\n        \n        # Flatten history: (B, H * 2 * d_max)\n        z_history_flat = z_history.reshape(-1, self.h * 2 * self.d_max)\n        \n        # Predict both streams\n        pred = self.net(z_history_flat) # (B, 2 * d_max)\n        \n        # Split into coord and dyn predictions\n        pred_coord, pred_dyn = torch.split(pred, self.d_max, dim=-1)\n        \n        # Zero out inactive dimensions in the outputs\n        out_mask = torch.zeros_like(pred_coord)\n        out_mask[:, :d_t] = 1.0\n        \n        pred_coord_active = pred_coord * out_mask\n        pred_dyn_active = pred_dyn * out_mask\n        \n        return pred_coord_active, pred_dyn_active\n\n\nclass DualStreamJEPASpatial(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, d_max=8, h=3, k=4, cooldown=300, stabilization_period=100):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.d_max = d_max\n        self.h = h\n        self.k = k\n        self.cooldown = cooldown\n        self.stabilization_period = stabilization_period\n        \n        self.encoder = DualStreamEncoder(d_max=d_max)\n        self.predictor = DualStreamPredictor(d_max=d_max, h=h)\n        \n        # shape (B, d_max)\n    return x_mean, var\n\ndef add_positional_encoding(x, pos_encoding="none"):\n    if pos_encoding == "none":\n        return x\n    B, _, W = x.shape\n    device = x.device\n    coords = torch.arange(W, dtype=torch.float32, device=device)\n    if pos_encoding == "linear":\n        pos_linear = coords / max(1.0, float(W - 1))\n        pos_linear = pos_linear.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, 1, -1)\n        return torch.cat([x, pos_linear], dim=1)\n    elif pos_encoding == "sinusoidal":\n        pos_sin_10 = torch.sin(coords / 10.0)\n        pos_cos_10 = torch.cos(coords / 10.0)\n        pos_sin_100 = torch.sin(coords / 100.0)\n        pos_cos_100 = torch.cos(coords / 100.0)\n        pos_embeds = torch.stack([pos_sin_10, pos_cos_10, pos_sin_100, pos_cos_100], dim=0)\n        pos_embeds = pos_embeds.unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1, -1)\n        return torch.cat([x, pos_embeds], dim=1)\n    return x\n\nclass DualStreamEncoder(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, d_max=8):\n        """\n        1D CNN mapping input of shape (B, 3, 128) to decoupled coordinate and dynamics streams.\n        """\n        super().__init__()\n        self.d_max = d_max\n        self.conv1 = nn.Conv1d(3, 16, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        self.conv2 = nn.Conv1d(16, 32, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        self.conv3 = nn.Conv1d(32, 64, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        self.conv4 = nn.Conv1d(64, 128, kernel_size=5, stride=2, padding=2)\n        \n        #
